### Privileges
# Nobles


country_event = {
	id = Prov_UI.011
	title = Prov_NOPrivilegesGrant
	desc = { trigger = { ai = no } desc = Prov_NOPrivilegesGrant_desc }
	desc = { trigger = { ai = yes } desc = no_localisation }
	picture = { trigger = { technology_group = western } picture = WE_GN_eventPicture }
	picture = { trigger = { OR = { technology_group = muslim technology_group = ottoman technology_group = steppestech } } picture = ISL_GN_eventPicture }
	picture = { trigger = { OR = { technology_group = eastern technology_group = byzantine } } picture = EE_GN_eventPicture }
	picture = { trigger = { technology_group = indian } picture = IND_GN_eventPicture }
	picture = { trigger = { OR = { technology_group = chinese technology_group = japanese technology_group = southeast_asian technology_group = tibetan } } picture = EA_GN_eventPicture }
	picture = { trigger = { OR = { technology_group = soudantech technology_group = sub_saharan technology_group = central_african technology_group = malagasy_tech technology_group = east_african } } picture = AFR_GN_eventPicture }
	picture = { trigger = { OR = { technology_group = austranesian technology_group = hawaii_tech } } picture = SEA_GN_eventPicture }
	picture = { trigger = { OR = { technology_group = mesoamerican technology_group = south_american } } picture = AMR_GN_eventPicture }

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}
	
	trigger = {
		check_key = { lhs = Prov_NOPow value = 1 }
	}
	
	immediate = {
	}

	option = {
		name = PTM_EXIT
		trigger = {
			ai = yes
		}
		highlight = yes
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 4.5
		}
	}
	# Noble Dues
	option = {
		name = Privilege_NOTaxERaise_title
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					ai = no
					Privilege_NotHas = { Privilege=NOTaxE Lvl=5 }
				}
				AND = {
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOTaxE Lvl=0 }
					Privilege_NOTaxE1CanRaise = yes
				}
				AND = {
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOTaxE Lvl=1 }
					Privilege_NOTaxE2CanRaise = yes
				}
				AND = {
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOTaxE Lvl=2 }
					Privilege_NOTaxE3CanRaise = yes
				}
				AND = {
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOTaxE Lvl=3 }
					Privilege_NOTaxE4CanRaise = yes
				}
				AND = {
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOTaxE Lvl=4 }
					Privilege_NOTaxE5CanRaise = yes
				}
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ProvUI_Action
		Privilege_NOTaxERaise = yes
		log ="SYS-Politics:[GetYear]:[This.GetName]:granted Noble Dues Privilege to:Nobles:+:[This.GovernmentName]"
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0 #stopped for AI, as restoring the previous level costs way more from what is gained when such exemption is granted
		}
	}
	# Noble Service
	option = {
		name = Privilege_NOServiceERaise_title
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					ai = no
					Privilege_NotHas = { Privilege=NOServiceE Lvl=5 }
				}
				AND = {
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOServiceE Lvl=0 }

				}
				AND = {

					Privilege_NOServiceE3CanRaise = yes
				}
				AND = {
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOServiceE Lvl=3 }
					Privilege_NOServiceE4CanRaise = yes
				}
				AND = {
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOServiceE Lvl=4 }
					Privilege_NOServiceE5CanRaise = yes
				}
			}
		}
		log ="SYS-Politics:[GetYear]:[This.GetName]:increased Noble Service Privilege to:Nobles"
		set_country_flag = ProvUI_Action
		Privilege_NOServiceERaise = { costs = yes }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0 #stopped for AI, as restoring the previous level costs way more from what is gained when such exemption is granted
		}
	}
	# Processing Monopoly
	option = {
		name = Privilege_NOProcessingRaise_title
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					ai = no
					Privilege_NotHas = { Privilege=NOProcessing Lvl=2 }
				}
				AND = {
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOProcessing Lvl=0 }
					Privilege_NOProcessing1CanRaise = yes
				}
				AND = {
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOProcessing Lvl=1 }
					Privilege_NOProcessing2CanRaise = yes
				}
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ProvUI_Action
		Privilege_NOProcessingRaise = yes
		log ="SYS-Politics:[GetYear]:[This.GetName]:increased Processing Monopoly Privilege to:Nobles"
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0 #stopped for AI, as restoring the previous level costs way more from what is gained when such exemption is granted

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					NOT = {has_country_flag = 	AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_HighInterest}
					has_country_flag = 	AI_Interaction_DecPowNO_HighInterest
					has_country_flag = 	AI_Interaction_DecPowNO_MedInterest
				}
			}
		}
	}

		name = Privilege_BGConsumerGoodsRaise_title
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					ai = no
					Privilege_NotHas = { Privilege=BGConsumerGoods Lvl=2 }
				}
				AND = {
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=BGConsumerGoods Lvl=0 }
					Privilege_BGConsumerGoods1CanRaise = yes
				}
				AND = {
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=BGConsumerGoods Lvl=1 }
					Privilege_BGConsumerGoods2CanRaise = yes
				}
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ProvUI_Action
		Privilege_BGConsumerGoodsRaise = yes
		log ="SYS-Politics:[GetYear]:[This.GetName]:increased Consumer Goods Monopoly Privilege to:Burghers"
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0 #stopped for AI, as restoring the previous level costs way more from what is gained when such exemption is granted
		}
	}

			ai = yes
		}

			factor = 4.5
		}

	# Sale of Office
	option = {
		name = Privilege_BUOfficeRaise_title
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					ai = no
					Privilege_NotHas = { Privilege=BUOffice Lvl=2 }
				}
				AND = {
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=BUOffice Lvl=0 }
					Privilege_BUOffice1CanRaise = yes
				}
				AND = {
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=BUOffice Lvl=1 }
					Privilege_BUOffice2CanRaise = yes
				}
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ProvUI_Action
		Privilege_BUOfficeRaise = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				ai = yes
				OR = {
					AND = {
						Privilege_NotHas = { Privilege=BUOffice Lvl=0 }
						NOT = { faction_influence = { faction = FC_BU influence = 30 } }
					}
					NOT = { faction_influence = { faction = FC_BU influence = 20 } }
				}
			}
			#boost BU faction
			BU_FactionBoost = yes
			log = "SYS-Politics:[GetYear]:[This.GetName]:Boost Faction:Bureaucrats:+:[This.GovernmentName]:::for priv"
			add_adm_power = -10
			add_dip_power = -10
			Pol_FacInfReactPosMajor = { Fac=BU }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { check_key = { lhs = Prov_BUPow value = 20 } }
				}
				Stab_Subtract25Percent = yes
			}
			set_key = { lhs = Prov_React value = 3 }
			set_key = { lhs = Prov_ReactSrc value = 5 }
			#country_event = { id = Prov_React.001 }
		}
		log ="SYS-Politics:[GetYear]:[This.GetName]:revoked Sale of Office Privilege (more formal) to:Bureaucrats:+:[This.GovernmentName]"
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 20

			modifier = { 
				factor = 0
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = AI_Reform_BUOffice
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecCrptLaws_HighInterest
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecCrptLaws_MedInterest
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecCrptLaws_LowInterest
				}
			}

			modifier = { 
				factor = 1.5
				has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecCrptLaws_HighInterest
			}

			modifier = { 
				factor = 0.75
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = AI_Reform_BUOffice
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecCrptLaws_HighInterest
				}
			}
			
			modifier = { 
				factor = 0.2
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = AI_Reform_BUOffice
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecCrptLaws_HighInterest
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecCrptLaws_MedInterest
				}
			}

			modifier = { 
				factor = 0.1
				AI_Interaction_stability_MedInterest = yes #interaction causes -1 stability hit
			}

			modifier = { 
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					AI_Interaction_stability_HighInterest = yes #interaction causes -1 stability hit
					Rights_AIReadyToReform = yes
					check_key = { lhs = Prov_BULoy value = 65 } #AI should bring it down with anti-corruption measures first
					corruption = 60 #AI should bring it down with anti-corruption measures first
					NOT = { check_key = { lhs = Prov_NOLoy value = 40 } } #only small hit to loyalty
					NOT = { check_key = { lhs = NO_RelationState value = -30 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}

		if = {
			limit = {
				ai = yes
				OR = {
					AND = {
						Privilege_Has = { Privilege=BUOversight Lvl=0 }
						NOT = { faction_influence = { faction = FC_BU influence = 30 } }
					}
					AND = {
						Privilege_Has = { Privilege=BUOversight Lvl=1 }
						NOT = { faction_influence = { faction = FC_BU influence = 40 } }
					}
				}
			}
			#boost BU faction
			BU_FactionBoost = yes
			log = "SYS-Politics:[GetYear]:[This.GetName]:Boost Faction:Bureaucrats:+:[This.GovernmentName]:::for priv"
			add_adm_power = -10
			add_dip_power = -10
			Pol_FacInfReactPosMajor = { Fac=BU }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { check_key = { lhs = Prov_BUPow value = 20 } }
				}
				Stab_Subtract25Percent = yes
			}
			set_key = { lhs = Prov_React value = 3 }
			set_key = { lhs = Prov_ReactSrc value = 5 }
			#country_event = { id = Prov_React.001 }
		}
		log ="SYS-Politics:[GetYear]:[This.GetName]:revoked Institutional Oversight Privilege (more formal) to:Bureaucrats:+:[This.GovernmentName]"

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 10

			modifier = { 
				factor = 0
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = AI_Reform_BUOversight
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecCrptLaws_HighInterest
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecCrptLaws_MedInterest
					current_income_balance = 0
				}
			}
			modifier = { 
				factor = 1.1
				has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecCrptLaws_HighInterest
			}
			modifier = { 
				factor = 0.1
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = AI_Reform_BUOversight
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecCrptLaws_HighInterest
					AND = {
						has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecCrptLaws_MedInterest
						current_income_balance = 0
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				AI_Interaction_stability_MedInterest = yes #interaction causes -1 stability hit
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					AI_Interaction_stability_HighInterest = yes #interaction causes -1 stability hit
					Rights_AIReadyToReform = yes
					check_key = { lhs = Prov_BULoy value = 70 } #AI should bring it down with anti-corruption measures first, it gives a temporary increase
					corruption = 50
					NOT = { check_key = { lhs = Prov_NOLoy value = 55 } } 
					NOT = { check_key = { lhs = NO_RelationState value = -30 } } 
				}
			}
		}
	}

				factor = 0
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = AI_Reform_BUEnlist
					AND = { #first level of the privilege is useful if NO Power is already below 70%, as country is already quite reformed and increase to tax efficiency is worth increase to elite power
						Privilege_Has = { Privilege=BUEnlist Lvl=0 }
						NOT = { check_key = { lhs = Prov_NOPow value = 70 } }
						check_key = { lhs = Prov_BUPow value = 40 }
					}
					AND = { #first level of the privilege is useful if state reach is getting too high, as it may stop other reforms
						Privilege_Has = { Privilege=BUEnlist Lvl=0 }
						check_key = { lhs = Prov_BUPow value = 55 }
					}
					AND = { #second level of the privilege is useful if NO Power is already below 60%, as country is already quite reformed and increase to tax efficiency is worth increase to elite power
						Privilege_Has = { Privilege=BUEnlist Lvl=1 }
						NOT = { check_key = { lhs = Prov_NOPow value = 60 } }
						check_key = { lhs = Prov_BUPow value = 60 }
					}
				}
			}
			
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {				
					Rights_AIReadyToReform = yes
					check_key = { lhs = Prov_BULoy value = 70 } #AI should bring it down with anti-corruption measures first, it gives a temporary increase
					corruption = 50
				}
			}
		}

# Nobles

country_event = {
	id = Prov_UI.021
	title = Prov_NOPrivilegesRevoke
	desc = { trigger = { ai = no } desc = Prov_NOPrivilegesRevoke_desc }
	desc = { trigger = { ai = yes } desc = no_localisation }
	picture = { trigger = { technology_group = western } picture = WE_GN_eventPicture }
	picture = { trigger = { OR = { technology_group = muslim technology_group = ottoman technology_group = steppestech } } picture = ISL_GN_eventPicture }
	picture = { trigger = { OR = { technology_group = eastern technology_group = byzantine } } picture = EE_GN_eventPicture }
	picture = { trigger = { technology_group = indian } picture = IND_GN_eventPicture }
	picture = { trigger = { OR = { technology_group = chinese technology_group = japanese technology_group = southeast_asian technology_group = tibetan } } picture = EA_GN_eventPicture }
	picture = { trigger = { OR = { technology_group = soudantech technology_group = sub_saharan technology_group = central_african technology_group = malagasy_tech technology_group = east_african } } picture = AFR_GN_eventPicture }
	picture = { trigger = { OR = { technology_group = austranesian technology_group = hawaii_tech } } picture = SEA_GN_eventPicture }
	picture = { trigger = { OR = { technology_group = mesoamerican technology_group = south_american } } picture = AMR_GN_eventPicture }

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}
	
	trigger = {
		check_key = { lhs = Prov_NOPow value = 1 }
	}
	
	immediate = {

	}
	option = {
		name = PTM_EXIT
		highlight = yes
		trigger = {
			ai = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 3
			modifier = { #temporary, will be moved to each option
				factor = 100
				Rights_AIReadyToReform = yes 
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 5
				is_at_war = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 3
				is_at_war = yes
				Rights_NotHasGreater = { Right=NOSer Lvl=2 }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				is_at_war = yes
				Rights_NotHasGreater = { Right=NOSer Lvl=3 }
			}
		}
	}
	# Noble Dues
	option = {
		name = Privilege_NOTaxELower_title
		trigger = {
   			Privilege_HasGreater = { Privilege=NOTaxE Lvl=1 }
			OR = {
				AND = {
					ai = no
					Privilege_HasGreater = { Privilege=NOTaxE Lvl=1 }
				}
				AND = {
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOTaxE Lvl=1 }
					Privilege_NOTaxE0CanLower = yes
				}
				AND = {
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOTaxE Lvl=2 }
					Privilege_NOTaxE1CanLower = yes
				}
				AND = {
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOTaxE Lvl=3 }
					Privilege_NOTaxE2CanLower = yes
				}
				AND = {
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOTaxE Lvl=4 }
					Privilege_NOTaxE3CanLower = yes
				}
				AND = {
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOTaxE Lvl=5 }
					Privilege_NOTaxE4CanLower = yes
				}
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ProvUI_Action
		Privilege_NOTaxELower = yes
		log ="SYS-Politics:[GetYear]:[This.GetName]:reduced Noble Dues Privilege to:Nobles"

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 5 #high factor as loyalty check comes from AI_Interaction_IncLoyNo flag

			modifier = { 
				factor = 0 #or a loyal estate
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = AI_Reform_NOTaxE
						has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecPowNO_HighInterest
						AND = { #a loyal estate accepts an occasional reduction
							NOT = { has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_HighInterest }
							NOT = { has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_MedInterest }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = { 
				factor = 0.05 #only a loyal estate, no pressing reason: reduce occasionally
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = AI_Reform_NOTaxE
						has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecPowNO_HighInterest
					}
				}
			}

			modifier = { 
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_HighInterest
					AI_Interaction_stability_HighInterest = yes
				}
			}
			modifier = { 
				factor = 0.4
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_MedInterest
					AI_Interaction_stability_MedInterest = yes
				}
			}	
			modifier = { 
				factor = 0.6
				OR = {
					AI_Interaction_stability_LowInterest = yes
				}
			}
			modifier = { 
				factor = 0.4
				#not applied when only a loyal estate is the reason
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = AI_Reform_NOTaxE
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecPowNO_HighInterest
				}
				Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOTaxE Lvl=2 } #if obligations are already moderate (2), something else should be targetted
			}
			modifier = { 
				factor = 0.1
				#not applied when only a loyal estate is the reason
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = AI_Reform_NOTaxE
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecPowNO_HighInterest
				}
				Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOTaxE Lvl=1 } #if obligations are already high (1), something else should be targetted

						has_country_flag = AI_Reform_NODynastic
						has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_corruption_HighInterest

### Elite Interactions
# Noble interactions
country_event = {
	id = Prov_UI.040
	title = Prov_UI.040.t
	desc = { trigger = { ai = no } desc = Prov_UI.040.d }
	desc = { trigger = { ai = yes } desc = no_localisation }
	picture = { trigger = { technology_group = western } picture = WE_GN_eventPicture }
	picture = { trigger = { OR = { technology_group = muslim technology_group = ottoman technology_group = steppestech } } picture = ISL_GN_eventPicture }
	picture = { trigger = { OR = { technology_group = eastern technology_group = byzantine } } picture = EE_GN_eventPicture }
	picture = { trigger = { technology_group = indian } picture = IND_GN_eventPicture }
	picture = { trigger = { OR = { technology_group = chinese technology_group = japanese technology_group = southeast_asian technology_group = tibetan } } picture = EA_GN_eventPicture }
	picture = { trigger = { OR = { technology_group = soudantech technology_group = sub_saharan technology_group = central_african technology_group = malagasy_tech technology_group = east_african } } picture = AFR_GN_eventPicture }
	picture = { trigger = { OR = { technology_group = austranesian technology_group = hawaii_tech } } picture = SEA_GN_eventPicture }
	picture = { trigger = { OR = { technology_group = mesoamerican technology_group = south_american } } picture = AMR_GN_eventPicture }

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}
	
	trigger = {
		check_key = { lhs = Prov_NOPow value = 1 }
	}
	
	immediate = {
		NO_Action_Setup = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = PTM_EXIT
		trigger = {
			ai = yes
		}
		highlight = yes
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1.5
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.666
				NOT = { check_key = { lhs = Prov_NOLoy value = 50 } }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				NOT = { check_key = { lhs = Prov_NOLoy value = 20 } }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = NO_FactionBoost_t
		trigger = {
			ai = no
			NO_Action_FactionBoostAllowed = yes
		}

		NO_Action_FactionBoost = yes
		log ="SYS-Politics:[GetYear]:[This.GetName]:Boost Faction:Nobles:+:[This.GovernmentName]"

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				check_key = { lhs = Prov_BUPow value = 25 }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				check_key = { lhs = Prov_BUPow value = 20 }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				check_key = { lhs = Prov_BUPow value = 15 }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				check_key = { lhs = Prov_BUPow value = 10 }
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				# if there is no need to increase loyalty, do not use the interaction
				NOT = { has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_HighInterest }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_MedInterest }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_LowInterest }
			}
			
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				Rights_AIReadyToReform = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				faction_influence = {
					faction = FC_NO
					influence = 85
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				faction_influence = {
					faction = FC_NO
					influence = 75
				}
			}
		}
	}

	#Provide Land Grants
	option = {
		name = NO_LandGrant_t
		trigger = {
			ai = no
			NO_Action_LandGrantAllowed = yes
		}
		NO_Action_LandGrant = yes

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0
		}
	}
	
	#Promote Lesser Nobility
	option = {
		name = NO_RecruitLords_t
		trigger = {
			ai = no
			NO_Action_RecruitLordsAllowed = yes
		}

		log ="SYS-Politics:[GetYear]:[This.GetName]:Promote Lesser Nobility:Nobles:+:[This.GovernmentName]"
		NO_Action_RecruitLords = yes

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0
		}
	}

	#Overlook Tenant Abuse
	option = {
		name = NO_Serfs_t
		trigger = {
			NO_Action_SerfsAllowed = yes
		}

		log ="SYS-Politics:[GetYear]:[This.GetName]:Overlook Tenant Abuse:Nobles:+:[This.GovernmentName]"

		NO_Action_Serfs = yes

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0.2

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = { #avoid giving more power to nobles
					AND = { # Dont use if: high desire for power decrease + high desire for loyalty
						has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecPowNO_HighInterest
						NOT = { has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_HighInterest }
					}
					AND = { # Dont use if: medium desire for power decrease + high/medium desire for loyalty
						has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecPowNO_MedInterest
						NOT = { has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_HighInterest }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_MedInterest }
					}
					AND = { # if there is no need to increase loyalty, do not use the interaction
						NOT = { has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_HighInterest }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_MedInterest }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_LowInterest }
					}
				}
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 2.5
				has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_HighInterest
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 1.75
				has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_MedInterest
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 1.1
				has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_LowInterest
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 2.5
				AI_Interaction_stability_HighInterest = yes
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				AI_Interaction_stability_MedInterest = yes
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				AI_Interaction_stability_LowInterest = yes
			}
		}
	}

	#Marry Noble Consort
	option = {
		name = NO_RoyalConsort_t
		trigger = {
			NO_Action_RoyalConsortAllowed = yes
		}
		log ="SYS-Politics:[GetYear]:[This.GetName]:Marry Noble Consort:Nobles:+:[This.GovernmentName]"

		NO_Action_RoyalConsort = yes

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 2
			
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				NOT = { stability = 0 }
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 3
				Rights_AIReadyToReform = yes
			}
		}
	}


	#Arrange a Strategic Marriage
	option = {
		name = NO_StrategicMarriage_t
		trigger = {
			NO_Action_StrategicMarriageAllowed = yes
		}

		log ="SYS-Politics:[GetYear]:[This.GetName]:Arrange a Strategic Marriage:Nobles:+:[This.GovernmentName]"

		NO_Action_StrategicMarriage = yes

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1.5

			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				OR = { #avoid giving more power to nobles
					AND = {
						has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecPowNO_HighInterest
						NOT = {has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_HighInterest}
						NOT = {has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_MedInterest}
						NOT = { Rights_AIReadyToReform = yes }
					}
					AND = {# if there is no need to increase loyalty, do not use the interaction
						NOT = {has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_HighInterest}
						NOT = {has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_MedInterest}
						NOT = {has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_LowInterest}
						NOT = { Rights_AIReadyToReform = yes }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}


	#Disown an Unpopular Relative
	option = {
		name = NO_DisownRelative_t
		trigger = {
			NO_Action_DisownRelativeAllowed = yes
		}
		log ="SYS-Politics:[GetYear]:[This.GetName]:Disown an Unpopular Relative:Nobles:+:[This.GovernmentName]"

		NO_Action_DisownRelative = yes

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1

			modifier = {
				factor = 0 #avoid loosing legitimacy if it is seeked
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_legitimacy_equivalent_HighInterest
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_legitimacy_equivalent_MedInterest
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_legitimacy_equivalent_LowInterest
				}
				# if there is no need to increase loyalty, do not use the interaction
				NOT = {has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_HighInterest}
			}
			
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_MedInterest
				NOT = { Rights_AIReadyToReform = yes }
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_LowInterest
				NOT = { Rights_AIReadyToReform = yes }
			}
			
			modifier = {
				factor = 3
				Rights_AIReadyToReform = yes
			}
		}
	}

	picture = { trigger = { OR = { technology_group = chinese technology_group = japanese technology_group = southeast_asian technology_group = tibetan technology_group = ottoman technology_group = western technology_group = eastern technology_group = byzantine technology_group = indian technology_group = steppestech } } picture = MON_TRI_eventPicture }

				NOT = {AI_Interaction_stability_MedInterest = yes}
				NOT = {AI_Interaction_stability_LowInterest = yes}

				NOT = {has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyTR_MedInterest}
				NOT = {has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyTR_LowInterest}
			}
			
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				# if there is dire need to increase stability or clan unity, use the interaction
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_legitimacy_equivalent_HighInterest
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyTR_HighInterest

# Bureaucrat interactions
country_event = {
	id = Prov_UI.044
	title = Prov_UI.044.t
	desc = { trigger = { ai = no } desc = Prov_UI.044.d }
	desc = { trigger = { ai = yes } desc = no_localisation }
	picture = ECONOMY_eventPicture

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}
	
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			ai = no
			check_key = { lhs = Prov_BUPow value = 1 }
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
		BU_Action_Setup = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = PTM_EXIT
		highlight = yes
		trigger = {
			ai = yes
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1.5
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = BU_FactionBoost_t
		trigger = {
			ai = no
			BU_Action_FactionBoostAllowed = yes
		}
		log ="SYS-Politics:[GetYear]:[This.GetName]:Boost Faction:Bureaucrats:+:[This.GovernmentName]"
		BU_Action_FactionBoost = yes

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0 #faction boosting moved to effect of actions requiring specific level of BU influence
		}
	}

	#Increase State Reach
	option = {
		name = BU_Develop_t
		trigger = {
			BU_Action_DevelopAllowed = yes
		}
		log ="SYS-Politics:[GetYear]:[This.GetName]:Increase State Reach:Bureaucrats:+:[This.GovernmentName]"
		BU_Action_Develop = yes
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1

			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				NOT = { stability = 1 }#Less chance for state reach increase if stability = 0, no penalty if 1 or higher
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecCrptLaws_MedInterest #Less chance if AI wants to limit part of provincial corruption comming from state reach
			}
			
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.75
				has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecCrptLaws_LowInterest #Less chance if AI wants to limit part of provincial corruption comming from state reach
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					NOT = { stability = 0 } #No chance for state reach increase if stability < 0
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecCrptLaws_HighInterest
					AND = { #Tribes are powerful and not loyal
						NOT = { check_key = { lhs = Prov_TRLoy value = 40 } }
						check_key = { lhs = Prov_TRPow value = 40 }
					}
				}
			}
			
			modifier = { #timmy wont do this
				factor = 0
				Timur_Has = yes
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 3.5 #high interest
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncPowBU_HighInterest
					AND = {
						has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncPowBU_MedInterest
						has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecPowNO_HighInterest					
					}
				}
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 2 #medium interest
				OR = {
					AND = {
						has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncPowBU_MedInterest
						NOT = { has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecPowNO_HighInterest }
					}
					AND = {
						has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncPowBU_LowInterest
						has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecPowNO_HighInterest
					}
				}
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = AI_Reform_BUTax
					has_country_flag = AI_Reform_BUDir
					has_country_flag = AI_Reform_BUInd
					has_country_flag = AI_Reform_BUSer
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncPowBU_HighInterest
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncPowBU_MedInterest
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncPowBU_LowInterest
				}
			}
		}
	}

	#Empower Local Traders
	option = {
		name = BU_EmpowerLocalTraders_t
		trigger = {
			BU_Action_EmpowerTradersAllowed = yes
		}
		log ="SYS-Politics:[GetYear]:[This.GetName]:Empower Local Traders:Bureaucrats:+:[This.GovernmentName]"
		BU_Action_EmpowerTraders = yes

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0.2

			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				any_owned_province = {
					Post_Has = yes
					OR = {
						check_key = { lhs = Post_S0Influence value = 45 }
						check_key = { lhs = Post_S1Influence value = 45 }
						check_key = { lhs = Post_S2Influence value = 45 }
						check_key = { lhs = Post_S3Influence value = 45 }
						check_key = { lhs = Post_S4Influence value = 45 }
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				any_owned_province = {
					Post_Has = yes
					OR = {
						check_key = { lhs = Post_S0Influence value = 35 }
						check_key = { lhs = Post_S1Influence value = 35 }
						check_key = { lhs = Post_S2Influence value = 35 }
						check_key = { lhs = Post_S3Influence value = 35 }
						check_key = { lhs = Post_S4Influence value = 35 }
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				any_owned_province = {
					Post_Has = yes
					OR = {
						check_key = { lhs = Post_S0Influence value = 25 }
						check_key = { lhs = Post_S1Influence value = 25 }
						check_key = { lhs = Post_S2Influence value = 25 }
						check_key = { lhs = Post_S3Influence value = 25 }
						check_key = { lhs = Post_S4Influence value = 25 }
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				any_owned_province = {
					Post_Has = yes
					OR = {
						check_key = { lhs = Post_S0Influence value = 15 }
						check_key = { lhs = Post_S1Influence value = 15 }
						check_key = { lhs = Post_S2Influence value = 15 }
						check_key = { lhs = Post_S3Influence value = 15 }
						check_key = { lhs = Post_S4Influence value = 15 }
					}
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				NOT = { stability = 1 }#Less chance for state reach increase if stability = 0, no penalty if 1 or higher
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				NOT = { stability = 1 }#Less chance for state reach increase if stability = 0, no penalty if 1 or higher
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				years_of_income = 10
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				OR = {
					NOT = { years_of_income = 3 }
					NOT = { treasury = 50 }
					num_of_loans = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = BU_Advisor_t
		trigger = {
			ai = no
			BU_Action_AdvisorAllowed = yes
		}
		log ="SYS-Politics:[GetYear]:[This.GetName]:Hire Advisor:Bureaucrats:+:[This.GovernmentName]"
		BU_Action_Advisor = yes

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = BU_AntiCorruption_t
		trigger = {
			ai = no
			NOT = { has_country_modifier = BU_CrptCD }
		}
		custom_tooltip = BU_AntiCorruption_desc
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = BU_CrptCD }
			}
			country_event = { id = Prov_UI.046 }
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip = BU_AntiCorruptionCD
		}
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = PTM_EXIT
		highlight = yes
		
		trigger = {
			ai = no
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
		}
	}

				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = ProvUI_Action
			Nat_Update = yes
			Unrest_Update = yes
			AI_Interactions_Recalc = yes
		}
	}

		days = 1

			ai = no
			check_key = { lhs = Prov_BUPow value = 1 }
		}
	}
	
	immediate = {
	}

	option = {
		name = PTM_EXIT
		highlight = yes
		
		trigger = {
			ai = yes

		ai_chance = {

		}
	}

		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0
		}
	}
	# "Provide Temporary Tax Relief"
	option = {
		name = BU_TaxRelief_t
		trigger = {
			BU_Action_TaxReliefAllowed = yes
		}
		log ="SYS-Politics:[GetYear]:[This.GetName]:Tax Relief:Commoners:+:[This.GovernmentName]"
		BU_Action_TaxRelief = yes
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0.5
			
			modifier = {
                factor = 2
                average_unrest = 1
            }
			
			modifier = { #do not gamble with income if you have professional army to pay for
				factor = 0.2
				Rights_HasGreater = { Right=NOSer Lvl=3 }
				NOT = { years_of_income = 10 }
			}
			
			modifier = { #limit usage if there is no need to increase stability
				factor = 0.2
				NOT = {				
					AI_Interaction_stability_HighInterest = yes
					AI_Interaction_stability_MedInterest = yes
				}
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				years_of_income = 10
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					NOT = { years_of_income = 3 }
					NOT = { treasury = 50 }
					num_of_loans = 1
				}
			}
			
			modifier = { #timmy wont do this
				factor = 0
				Timur_Has = yes
			}
		}
	}
	# "Provide Grain for the Commoners"
	option = {
		name = BU_Grain_t
		trigger = {
			BU_Action_GrainAllowed = yes
		}

		BU_Action_Grain = yes

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0.5
			
			modifier = {
                factor = 2
                average_unrest = 1
            }

			modifier = {
				factor = 5
				is_emperor_of_china = yes
				NOT = { imperial_mandate = 85 }
			}
			
			modifier = { #do not gamble with income if you have professional army to pay for
				factor = 0.2
				Rights_HasGreater = { Right=NOSer Lvl=3 }
				NOT = { years_of_income = 10 }
			}
			
			modifier = { #limit usage if there is no need to increase stability
				factor = 0.2
				NOT = {				
					AI_Interaction_stability_HighInterest = yes
					AI_Interaction_stability_MedInterest = yes

				NOT = {

				}
			}
		}
	}

	#Crackdown on Aristocratic Faction
	option = {
		name = NO_FactionCrackdown_t 
		trigger = {
			ai = no
			NO_Action_FactionCrackdownAllowed = yes
		}
		log ="SYS-Politics:[GetYear]:[This.GetName]:Crackdown on Faction:Nobles:-:[This.GovernmentName]"

		ai_chance = {

	}
	
	#Expropriate Land
	option = {
		name = NO_LandExpropriate_t
		trigger = {
			NO_Action_LandExpropriateAllowed = yes
		}
		NO_Action_LandExpropriate = yes
		
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			#previously modified by Prov_TRPow (high TRPow -> less interaction)
			#previously modified by Prov_NOLoy (high NOLoy -> more interaction)
			#previously modified by BU_PrpResources (high BU_PrpResources -> less interaction)
			#previously modified by Prov_NOPow (high Prov_NOPow -> more interaction)
			
			modifier = { 
				factor = 1.5
				stability = 2
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_at_war = yes
			}
			
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_HighInterest
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_MedInterest
					Rights_AIReadyToReform = yes
				}
			}
			
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.25
				has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_LowInterest				
			}
			
			#group of checks for NO being stripped from privileges supporting their high power
			modifier = {
				factor = 0 #no sense to demote if there is no taxation in place
				OR = {
					Rights_Has = { Right=BUTax Lvl=1 }
					Rights_Has = { Right=NOGov Lvl=1 }
				}
			}
			
			modifier = {
				factor = 0 #no sense to demote if there are all privileges to opress peasantry in place
				OR = {
					AND = {
						Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOProcessing Lvl=2 }
						Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOForestry Lvl=2 }
						Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOGame Lvl=2 }
					}
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOSeigneurial Lvl=2 }
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOSeigneurial Lvl=3 }
				}
			}
			
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				Rights_Has = { Right=NOGov Lvl=5 }
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 3 #high interest
				has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecPowNO_HighInterest
			}

			modifier = { 
			#do not use interaction if there is no need from AI interaction flags
			#meant to reduce NO Power
				factor = 0
				NOT = { 
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecPowNO_HighInterest
						has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecPowNO_MedInterest
						has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecPowNO_LowInterest
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	#Demote Greater Nobility
	option = {
		name = NO_DemoteLords_t
		trigger = {
			NO_Action_DemoteLordsAllowed = yes
		}
		log ="SYS-Politics:[GetYear]:[This.GetName]:Demote Greater Nobility:Nobles:-:[This.GovernmentName]"

		NO_Action_DemoteLords = yes

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1

			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				stability = 0 # stability at least 0
				NOT = { has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_HighInterest } # If in need of loyalty dont increase likelyhood
				OR = {
					AND = {
						### Able to do NOGov2->3 by privileges but power is too high
						Privilege_NotHasGreater = { Privilege=NOProcessing Lvl=2 }
						Privilege_NotHasGreater = { Privilege=NOGame Lvl=2 }
						Privilege_NotHasGreater = { Privilege=NOForestry Lvl=2 }
						Privilege_NotHasGreater = { Privilege=NOSeigneurial Lvl=3 }
						Privilege_NotHasGreater = { Privilege=NOAutonomy Lvl=3 }
						Privilege_NotHasGreater = { Privilege=NOTaxE Lvl=3 }
						Privilege_NotHasGreater = { Privilege=NOServiceE Lvl=3 }
						check_key = { lhs = Prov_NOPow value = 85 }
					}
					AND = {
						### Able to do NOGov3->4 by privileges but power is too high
						check_key = { lhs = Prov_NOPow value = 70 }
   						Privilege_NotHasGreater = { Privilege=NOSeigneurial Lvl=2 }
						Privilege_NotHasGreater = { Privilege=NOAutonomy Lvl=2 }
						Privilege_NotHasGreater = { Privilege=NOTaxE Lvl=1 }
						Privilege_NotHasGreater = { Privilege=NOServiceE Lvl=1 }
					}
					AND = {
						### Able to do NOSer1->2 by requirements but power is too high
						check_key = { lhs = Prov_NOPow value = 85 }
						mil_idea_group_amount = { amount = 1 }
						Privilege_HasGreater = { Privilege=BUStructure Lvl=1 }
						check_key = { lhs = Prov_BUPow value = 10 }
					}
					AND = {
						### Able to do NOSer2->3 by requirements but power is too high
						check_key = { lhs = Prov_NOPow value = 80 }
						mil_idea_group_amount = { amount = 2 }
						check_key = { lhs = Prov_BUPow value = 20 }
					}
				}
			}

			modifier = { # High interest in decreasing power, low interest in increasing loyalty
				factor = 1.25
				has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecPowNO_HighInterest
				stability = 1
				has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_LowInterest
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecPowNO_MedInterest
			}

			modifier = {
				factor = 0.1
				has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecPowNO_LowInterest
			}

			modifier = { 
				factor = 0.5
				NOT = { stability = 1 }
			}

			#do not use interaction if there is no need from AI interaction flags meant to reduce NO Power
			modifier = { 
				factor = 0
				NOT = { 
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecPowNO_HighInterest
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecPowNO_MedInterest
					has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_DecPowNO_LowInterest
				}
			}

			modifier = { ## Dont use if stability is below 0
				factor = 0
				NOT = { stability = 0 }
			}
			
			#group of checks for NO being stripped from privileges supporting their high power
			modifier = {
				factor = 0 #no sense to demote if there is no taxation in place
				OR = {
					Rights_Has = { Right=BUTax Lvl=1 }
					AND = { # If Gov1->2 reform cannot be done dont demote
						Rights_Has = { Right=NOGov Lvl=1 }
   						Privilege_HasGreater = { Privilege=NOHierarchy Lvl=3 }
					}
				}
			}
			
			modifier = {
				factor = 0 #no sense to demote if there are all privileges to opress peasantry in place
				OR = {
					AND = {
						Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOProcessing Lvl=2 }
						Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOForestry Lvl=2 }
						Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOGame Lvl=2 }
					}
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOSeigneurial Lvl=2 }
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOSeigneurial Lvl=3 }
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOTaxE Lvl=3 }
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOTaxE Lvl=4 }
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOServiceE Lvl=3 }
					Privilege_Has = { Privilege=NOServiceE Lvl=4 }
				}
			}
			
			#blockade when getting ready to enact a reform
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					AND = {
						OR = {
							has_country_flag = AI_Interaction_IncLoyNO_HighInterest 
							is_at_war = yes
						}
						Rights_NotHasGreater = { Right=NOSer Lvl=3 } #do not demote if your army relies on nobles (also because power will bounce right back)
					}
					Rights_AIReadyToReform = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

				NOT = { check_key = { lhs = Prov_CLLoy value = 40 } }

			Nat_Update = yes
			Unrest_Update = yes
			AI_Interactions_Recalc = yes
		}
	}

#Clergy interaction - religious conversions

country_event = {
	id = Prov_UI.436
	title = Prov_UI.436.title
	desc = { trigger = { ai = no } desc = Prov_UI.436.desc }
	desc = { trigger = { ai = yes } desc = no_localisation }
	picture = all_church_state

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			ReligionSize_Country = yes
			every_owned_province = {
				MN_Missionary = yes
			}
		}
	}
	
	option = {
		name = "Prov_UI.436.optaa" #Religion of the Realm
		trigger = { western_christian = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_key = { lhs = Prov_CLLoy value = 40 }
				NOT = { check_key = { lhs = Prov_CLPow value = 70 } }
				OR = { is_religion_enabled = waldensian is_religion_enabled = lollard is_religion_enabled = hussite is_religion_enabled = protestant is_religion_enabled = reformed }
			}
			custom_tooltip = Rights_UI_AllOf
			custom_tooltip = POP_CLHasLoy_40
			custom_tooltip = POP_CLNotHasPow_70
			custom_tooltip = DGRealmReligion
			custom_tooltip = realm_religion_tt
			hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = DG_Convert.301 } }
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip = Rights_UI_AllOf
			custom_tooltip = POP_CLHasLoy_40
			custom_tooltip = POP_CLNotHasPow_70
			custom_tooltip = DGRealmReligion
			custom_tooltip = realm_religion_tt
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "Prov_UI.436.optba" #Organise the Missionaries
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_key = { lhs = Prov_CLLoy value = 40 }
				NOT = { check_key = { lhs = Prov_CLPow value = 70 } }
				any_owned_province = {
					isValidProv = yes
					OR = {
						has_missionary = yes
						AND = {
							has_province_flag = UI_Select
							NOT = { religion = owner }
							check_key = { lhs = MN_S0MStrength value = 0.3 }
						}
						AND = {
							has_province_flag = UI_Select
							religion = owner
							OR = {
								check_key = { lhs = MN_S1MStrength value = 0.3 }
								check_key = { lhs = MN_S2MStrength value = 0.3 }
								check_key = { lhs = MN_S3MStrength value = 0.3 }
								check_key = { lhs = MN_S4MStrength value = 0.3 }
								check_key = { lhs = MN_S5MStrength value = 0.3 }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = Rights_UI_AllOf
			custom_tooltip = POP_CLHasLoy_40
			custom_tooltip = POP_CLNotHasPow_70
			custom_tooltip = DGMissionary
			custom_tooltip = missionary_organisation_tt
			hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = DG_Convert.101 } }
		}
		else = {
			custom_tooltip = Rights_UI_AllOf
			custom_tooltip = POP_CLHasLoy_40
			custom_tooltip = POP_CLNotHasPow_70
			custom_tooltip = DGMissionary
			custom_tooltip = missionary_organisation_tt
			hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = DG_Convert.101 } }
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "Prov_UI.436.optca" #Establlish an Inquisition
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_key = { lhs = Prov_CLLoy value = 40 }
				NOT = { check_key = { lhs = Prov_CLPow value = 70 } }
				NOT = { has_country_modifier = evangel_cm }
			}
			custom_tooltip = Rights_UI_AllOf
			custom_tooltip = POP_CLHasLoy_40
			custom_tooltip = POP_CLNotHasPow_70
			custom_tooltip = DGInquisition
			custom_tooltip = inquisition_organisation_tt
			hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = DG_Inquisition.001 } }
		}
		else = {

			custom_tooltip = POP_CLHasLoy_40
			custom_tooltip = POP_CLNotHasPow_70
			custom_tooltip = DGInquisition
			custom_tooltip = inquisition_organisation_tt
		}

	}
	option = {

country_event = {
	id = Prov_UI.437
	title = Prov_UI.437.title
	desc = { trigger = { ai = no } desc = Prov_UI.437.desc }
	desc = { trigger = { ai = yes } desc = no_localisation }
	picture = all_church_state

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	option = {
		name = "Prov_UI.437.optaa" #Indulgences
		trigger = {
			western_christian = yes
			NOT = { is_key_equal = { lhs = aspect_07 value = 3 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_key_equal = { lhs = aspect_07 value = 1 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { check_key = { lhs = Prov_CLPow value = 50 } }
					check_key = { lhs = Prov_BUPow value = 25 }
				}
				custom_tooltip = Rights_UI_AllOf
				custom_tooltip = POP_CLNotHasPow_50
				custom_tooltip = POP_BUHasPow_25
				custom_tooltip = indulgence_condemn_one_tt
				custom_tooltip = tenet_change_cost_tt
				Privilege_ManaCost = { adm_power=50 dip_power=250 }
				Public_ChangePowerbrokerLoyaltyTooltipProv = { Powerbroker=CL Amount=-20 }
				add_corruption = 5
				hidden_effect = { indulgence_condemnation_se = yes }
				if = {
					limit = { religion = catholic }
					if = {
						limit = { PAP = { is_key_equal = { lhs = aspect_07 value = 1 } } }
						PAP = { add_opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = change_tenet_hate } }
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { PAP = { is_key_equal = { lhs = aspect_07 value = 3 } } }
						PAP = { add_opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = change_tenet_thrilled } }
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { religion = avignonist }
					if = {
						limit = { AVI = { is_key_equal = { lhs = aspect_07 value = 1 } } }
						AVI = { add_opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = change_tenet_hate } }
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { AVI = { is_key_equal = { lhs = aspect_07 value = 3 } } }
						AVI = { add_opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = change_tenet_thrilled } }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { any_known_country = { religion = ROOT NOT = { tag = PAP } NOT = { tag = AVI } is_key_equal = { lhs = aspect_07 value = 1 } } }
					custom_tooltip = indulgence_condemn_negative_tt
					hidden_effect = {
						every_known_country = {
							limit = { religion = ROOT NOT = { tag = PAP } NOT = { tag = AVI } is_key_equal = { lhs = aspect_07 value = 1 } }
							add_opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = change_tenet_dislike }
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { any_known_country = { NOT = { tag = PAP } NOT = { tag = AVI } is_key_equal = { lhs = aspect_09 value = 3 } } }
					custom_tooltip = indulgence_condemn_positive_tt
					hidden_effect = {
						every_known_country = {
							limit = { is_key_equal = { lhs = aspect_07 value = 3 } }
							add_opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = change_tenet_like }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = Rights_UI_AllOf
				custom_tooltip = POP_CLNotHasPow_50
				custom_tooltip = POP_BUHasPow_25
				custom_tooltip = indulgence_condemn_one_tt
				custom_tooltip = tenet_change_cost_tt
				custom_tooltip = indulgence_cost_one_tt
				custom_tooltip = diplomatic_impact_tt
				if = {
					limit = { religion = catholic }
					if = { limit = { PAP = { is_key_equal = { lhs = aspect_07 value = 1 } } } custom_tooltip = indulgence_pope_negative_tt }
					else_if = { limit = { PAP = { is_key_equal = { lhs = aspect_07 value = 3 } } } custom_tooltip = indulgence_pope_positive_tt }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { religion = avignonist }
					if = { limit = { AVI = { is_key_equal = { lhs = aspect_07 value = 1 } } } custom_tooltip = indulgence_pope_negative_tt }
					else_if = { limit = { AVI = { is_key_equal = { lhs = aspect_07 value = 3 } } } custom_tooltip = indulgence_pope_positive_tt }
				}
				custom_tooltip = indulgence_condemn_negative_tt
				custom_tooltip = indulgence_condemn_positive_tt
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_key_equal = { lhs = aspect_07 value = 2 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { check_key = { lhs = Prov_CLPow value = 60 } }
					check_key = { lhs = Prov_BUPow value = 15 }
				}
				custom_tooltip = Rights_UI_AllOf
				custom_tooltip = POP_CLNotHasPow_60
				custom_tooltip = POP_BUHasPow_15
